# X2a_asset_catalogue_visual — Visual asset catalogue (textures, sprites, shapes, animations, terrain ramps, icons, thumbnails, help images, shaders, fonts)
Scope: every file in `data/textures/**` (incl. `unicode/`, `plusmenu/`), `data/sprites/**`, `data/shapes/**` (`.shp` and `source/*.max`), `data/animations/**` (`.bmp` + `.anim`), `data/terrain/**`, `data/icons/**`, `data/thumbnails/**`, `data/mwhelp/**`, `data/shaders/**`, `data/sepulveda/*.bmp` — 799 files. Font bitmaps live in `data/textures/unicode/` (live) and `data/textures/*font*.bmp` (legacy). Build baseline: see build_matrix.md (`Release|Win32` OpenGL + `TARGET_PC_FULLGAME`; secondary `ReleaseDirect3D`).

Reused and re-verified material: `OUT/_work/drafts/P12_render_core.md` §24.5, §24.7, §25.1–25.3 (decoder, upload, resource manager), `P12_shapes.csv` (superseded by `X2a_shapes.csv`, which uses a parser that follows `code/lib/shape.cpp` token-for-token). Generated tables: `OUT/_work/drafts/X2a_textures.csv` (527 rows: every bitmap, `.anim`, shader and `.psp`), `OUT/_work/drafts/X2a_shapes.csv` (272 rows: every `.shp` and `.max`), `OUT/_work/drafts/X2a_refs.csv` (every resolved reference: asset, kind, site `path:Symbol:Lline`, build, `#if` condition, reachability note, load flags, nearby GL state), `OUT/_work/drafts/X2a_missing.csv` (names requested that have no file). Script: `OUT/_work/scripts/X2a_catalogue.py` (run `python3 -I X2a_catalogue.py <SRC> <OUT/_work> <scratch>`).

## [→ 25_textures_and_assets] 25.0 Method and provenance

### How every file was characterised [C]
| Property | How obtained | Code it mirrors |
|---|---|---|
| Width, height, bit depth, header size, compression, palette | BMP file header + info header parsed exactly as the engine does (palette count = `(bfOffBits−54)/4` for 40-byte headers, `(bfOffBits−26)/3` for 12-byte OS/2 headers) | `code/lib/bitmap.cpp:BitmapRGBA::LoadBmp`, `ReadWinBMPInfoHeader`, `ReadOS2BMPInfoHeader`, `ReadBMPPalette` |
| Loader-compatibility problems | flagged when the engine would mis-decode: header ≠ 40/12 bytes, compression ≠ `BI_RGB`, bit depth ∉ {4,8,24,32}, negative (top-down) height, 4-bit width not a multiple of 8 or 8-bit width not a multiple of 4 (row padding is skipped only by `Read24BitLine`), pixel data not immediately after the palette (the loader never seeks to `bfOffBits`) | `Read4BitLine`, `Read8BitLine`, `Read24BitLine`, `Read32BitLine` |
| Alpha | 32-bit files: min/max of the A byte and % of fully transparent / partial pixels. Other depths have no alpha (loader sets A = 255) | `Read32BitLine` (BGRA), palette `a = 255` |
| Colour-key usage | count of exact (255,0,255) pixels — these become (128,128,128,0) only when the texture is requested with the mask flag | `BitmapRGBA::ConvertPinkToTransparent`, `Resource::ProcessBmp` |
| "Black background" | % of pure black pixels (art meant for additive `GL_SRC_ALPHA, GL_ONE` blending, where black is invisible) — descriptive only [I] | — |
| Power of two | both axes; non-power-of-two (NPOT) files are rescaled at upload whenever power-of-two is required (always under D3D) | `BitmapRGBA::ConvertToTexture` |
| `.shp` structure | tokenised with the `TextReader` separator set `" \t\n\r:,"`; fragments, parents, positions/colours/vertices counts, triangles (strip → triangle conversion dropping degenerate triangles, as the engine does), markers with parent and `Depth`, hierarchy/duplicate/marker-depth checks | `code/lib/shape.cpp:Shape::Load`, `ShapeFragment::ShapeFragment(TextReader*,…)`, `ParseStripBlock`, `ParseTriangleBlock`, `ShapeMarker::ShapeMarker(TextReader*,…)`; `contrib/systemiv/lib/filesys/text_stream_readers.cpp:DEFAULT_SEPERATOR_CHARS` |
| `.anim` | `WIDTH/HEIGHT/START/STOP`, object and keyframe counts, `IMAGENAME` values | `code/animatedpanel.cpp:AnimatedPanel` reader (`IMAGENAME` → `m_imageName`) |
| Packed archive | re-implementation of `tools/build_multiwinia_data.bat` + `tools/listfiles.pl` (exclusion globs case-insensitive, `**/` short excludes, directory pruning, `main.dat` extension filter `tex bmp jpg shp txt vs ps ps3 h anim`, `tools/init.list`, `tools/language.list` and the per-language `tools/*language.list`) | §25.3 |

### How references were resolved [C]
1. **String literals in code.** Every `.cpp/.h/.inc` under `code/` and `contrib/eclipse/` was lexed (strings, char literals, `//` and `/* */` comments distinguished), with `"…" TEXTURE_EXTENSION` folded to `"….bmp"` (`TEXTURE_EXTENSION` = `"bmp"`, `code/lib/universal_include.h`). For each literal the script records the enclosing symbol, the `#if` stack and whether the line is compiled in each configuration (FULL, D3D, DEBUG = `TARGET_DEBUG` macro set, EDITOR = `LOCATION_EDITOR`), and whether the file is in `Darwinia.vcproj` (`OUT/_work/manifest.csv`). A literal matches an asset when it equals the resource name (`data/` stripped, lower-cased — every `Resource` texture/shape lookup lower-cases the name), the name with `.bmp` appended, `shadow_<name>` (runtime blurred copy), or a bare `.shp` name (shapes are always looked up as `data/shapes/<name>`).
2. **Run-time-built names** — each rule is tied to the exact code site that formats the name and to a value set taken from code or data:

| Rule | Name pattern | Site(s) | Value set (source) |
|---|---|---|---|
| research-icon | `icons/icon_<name>.bmp` | `code/taskmanager_interface_multiwinia.cpp:TaskManagerInterfaceMultiwinia::TaskManagerInterfaceMultiwinia`, `RenderRunningTasks`; `code/markersystem.cpp:MarkerSystem::Render`; `code/gamecursor.cpp:GameCursor::RenderSelectedTaskIcon`; `code/taskmanager_interface_icons.cpp`, `code/taskmanager_interface_gestures.cpp` (legacy) | the 29 names of `code/global_world.cpp:GlobalResearch::GetTypeName` (`Task::GetTaskName` delegates to it): Darwinian, Officer, Squad, Laser, Grenade, Rocket, Controller, AirStrike, Armour, TaskManager, Engineer, Harvester, Nuke, Subversion, PersonalShield, HotFeet, GunTurret, Shaman, Infection, MagicalForest, DarkForest, AntNest, Plague, Egg, MeteorShower, FireExtinguisher, Rage, Tank, DropShip |
| research-gesture | `icons/gesture_<name>.bmp` | same task-manager constructors; `code/interface/gesture_window.cpp` (dead: `GESTURE_EDITOR && USE_SEPULVEDA_HELP_TUTORIAL`) | same 29 names (8 files exist) |
| crate-icon | `icons/icon_<crate>.bmp` | `code/interface/helpandoptions_windows.cpp:CrateButton::CrateButton` (only via `INCLUDE_CRATE_HELP_WINDOW`, debug) | `code/worldobject/crate.cpp:Crate::GetName` (Squad … SpeedUp, default "Crate") |
| icon-stem | `icons/<stem>.bmp` | stems passed as `iconKb/iconXbox/flagIcon` to `code/control_help.cpp:ControlHelpSystem::RenderControlHelp`/`RenderControlHelpDPad` and `code/multiwiniahelp.cpp:MultiwiniaHelp::RenderControlHelp(int,…)` | literal stems at the call sites |
| mode-image | `mwhelp/mode_<blueprint>.bmp` (lower-cased) | `code/UI/GameMenuWindow.cpp:GameMenuWindow::SetupGameSelectPage`, `SetupSPDemoPage` (preload loops over all blueprints); `code/UI/GameTypeImageButton.h:GameTypeImageButton::Render` | `GAMETYPE` names in `data/multiwinia.txt`: Domination, KingOfTheHill, CaptureTheStatue, Assault, RocketRiot, Blitzkrieg, TankBattle |
| help-general | `mwhelp/general<p>.bmp` | `code/interface/helpandoptions_windows.cpp:GeneralHelpWindow::Create` | p = 1…5 (`MAX_PAGES`) |
| help-gametype | `mwhelp/help_<t>_<p>.bmp` | `code/interface/helpandoptions_windows.cpp:GameTypeHelpWindow::Create` | (t,p) for which phrase `multiwinia_helppage_<t+1>_<p>` exists in `data/language/english.txt`: (0,1) (1,1–2) (2,1–3) (3,1–3) (4,1–4) (5,1–3) |
| map-thumbnail | `thumbnails/<level stem>.bmp` lower-cased | `code/UI/MapImageButton.h:MapImageButton::Render`, `code/UI/GameMenuWindow.cpp:GameMenuWindow::PreloadThumbnails` | every `data/levels/mp_*` file listed by `code/game_menu.cpp:GameMenu::CreateMapList` whose `GameTypes` names a permitted blueprint; TankBattle maps → B:DEBUG (`code/app.cpp:App::IsGameModePermitted`); `data/levels/old/` is never listed |
| terrain | `terrain/<landColourFile|wavesColourFile|waterColourFile>` | `code/landscape_renderer.cpp:LandscapeRenderer::LandscapeRenderer`, `code/water.cpp:Water::Water`, `Water::RenderFlatWater`, `code/mapfile.cpp:MapFile::SaveTextures` | level-file keys (`code/level_file.cpp:LevelFile::ParseLandscapeData`), defaults `landscape_default.bmp`/`waves_default.bmp`/`water_default.bmp` (`LevelFile::LevelFile`), Christmas overrides `landscape_icecaps.bmp`/`waves_earth.bmp`/`water_icecaps.bmp` |
| animation images | `animations/<IMAGENAME>` | `code/animatedpanel_renderer.cpp:AnimatedPanelRenderer` (GetBitmap + GetTexture) | `IMAGENAME` values in the six `.anim` files, which are loaded by `code/multiwiniahelp.cpp:MultiwiniaHelp::InitialiseAnimatedPanel` (one per game type) |
| font-atlas | `textures/unicode/<font>.<lo>.<hi>.bmp` | `code/lib/text_renderer.cpp:TextRenderer::BuildUnicodeArray` (`ListResources("textures/unicode/", "<font>.*.*.bmp")`) | fonts `speccy`, `editor`, `square` (`code/app.cpp:App::SetLanguage`) |
| cloud-face | `sepulveda/sepulveda<n>.bmp` | `code/clouds.cpp:Clouds::RenderFace` | n = 1–3 random, 4 (eyes shut), 5 (mouth open) |
| darwinian-ouch | `sprites/darwinian_ouch<n>.bmp` | `code/worldobject/darwinian.cpp:Darwinian::Render` | n = 1 + uid%10 when uid%10 < 3 → 1–3 (4 is a literal) |
| armyant-shape | `armyant<n>.shp` | `code/worldobject/armyant.cpp:ArmyAnt::SetShape` | n = 2, 3 |
| hlsl-include | `shaders/globals.h` | `code/lib/shader.cpp:MyD3DXInclude::Open` | `#include "globals.h"` in `data/shaders/deform{Diamond,Fullscreen,Sphere}.ps` |
| level shapes | `shapes/<name>.shp` | `code/worldobject/staticshape.cpp:StaticShape` (shape name read from the level) | `StaticShape` lines of loaded levels |

3. **Data references.** All `data/**/*.txt` and `*.anim` (except `data/language/`) were scanned for `*.bmp/*.shp/*.anim` tokens. Level files are classified: `data/levels/mp_*` with a permitted game type = live; TankBattle maps = B:DEBUG; `GameTypes none` maps (`mp_sandbox*`, `mp_benchmark_1`, `mp_assault_stress`) = special targets; `map_*`/`mission_*` = Darwinia [B:LEGACY]; `data/levels/old/` = never loaded. `data/input/*` icon bindings are parsed but never drawn (`code/lib/input/input.cpp:InputManager::controlIcon` has no caller). `data/purity/*` is never loaded.
4. **Reachability overrides** (compiled into FULL but cannot run in a Multiwinia match) — each verified by grep:

| Code | Tag | Reason |
|---|---|---|
| `code/taskmanager_interface_icons.cpp`, `code/taskmanager_interface_gestures.cpp` (all) | LEGACY | `code/taskmanager_interface.cpp:TaskManagerInterface::CreateTaskManager` creates `TaskManagerInterfaceMultiwinia` whenever `App::Multiplayer()`; the other two only in single-player |
| `code/shaman_interface.cpp` (all) | LEGACY | shown only from `TaskManagerInterfaceIcons` |
| `code/control_help.cpp` (all) | LEGACY | `ControlHelpSystem::Render` returns immediately when `App::Multiplayer()`; `RenderHelp` additionally requires `App::IsSinglePlayer()` (Campaign/Prologue) |
| `code/global_world.cpp` render code, `code/global_internet.cpp` | LEGACY | Darwinia world map (`Renderer` branch `m_locationId == -1`, P12 §24.2) |
| `code/multiwiniahelp.cpp:MultiwiniaHelp::RenderControlHelp()` (no-arg, L2781–2994), `RenderBasicControlHelp`, `RenderCurrentUnitHelp`, `RenderCurrentTaskHelp` | LEGACY | no live caller (the two task-manager calls are commented out) |
| `code/renderer.cpp:Renderer::RenderFrame` Darwin-logo block (L795–830) | LEGACY | `m_renderDarwinLogo` is set only by a script command (`code/script.cpp`) used by Darwinia cut-scenes |
| `code/renderer.cpp:Renderer::RenderFlatTexture` | LEGACY | its only call is commented out |
| `code/UI/GameTypeImageButton.h` prologue/campaign branch | LEGACY | `GAMETYPE_PROLOGUE/CAMPAIGN` highlighted only from `PageDarwinia` (P18) |
| `code/clouds.cpp:Clouds::RenderFace` | DEBUG | `m_faceTime` is set only by `Clouds::ShowDrSFace`, called only from `code/interface/cheat_window.cpp` |
| `code/interface/gesture_window.cpp` | DEBUG/DEAD | created only by `code/interface/debugmenu.cpp`; body under `GESTURE_EDITOR && USE_SEPULVEDA_HELP_TUTORIAL` |
| `code/interface/helpandoptions_windows.cpp:CrateButton`, `CrateHelpWindow` | DEBUG | reachable only through `CrateHelpWindowButton` (`#ifdef INCLUDE_CRATE_HELP_WINDOW`) |

Status vocabulary used everywhere below: **referenced** (≥1 confirmed reference from compiled code or a loaded data file; the bracket gives the best build in which it is reachable), **unused** (no reference, or references only in non-compiled/commented code, or a `.max`/`.psp` authoring source), **unknown** (only indirect evidence — after manual review none remain). Weak evidence (bare words equal to a file stem in unrelated code, generic `printf` patterns such as `"icons/%s.bmp"`) never makes a file "referenced"; every such case was reviewed by hand (six bare-word collisions are documented in the per-file tables).

## [→ 25_textures_and_assets] 25.1 Key findings

- **799 files in scope**: 667 BMP bitmaps, 139 `.shp` meshes, 133 `.max` sources, 6 `.anim` panel scripts, 13 HLSL shader files + 1 HLSL include, 1 Paint Shop Pro file (`data/icons/banner_template.psp`). Every BMP has a 40-byte `BITMAPINFOHEADER`; all are `BI_RGB` except **`data/textures/shaman_background.bmp` (RLE8, compression 1)**, which the loader cannot decode (debug assert; garbage in release) — it is used only by the legacy ShamanInterface [C].
- No shipped palettised bitmap violates the loader's row-padding limitation (4-bit widths are multiples of 8, 8-bit widths multiples of 4) and no file has a top-down height [C].
- **Usage in a baseline Multiwinia match**: 544 of the 666 non-source files are referenced [B:FULL] (per-directory counts in §25.4). The other 122: 71 unused (54 never named anywhere, 16 named only in non-compiled/commented code or never-loaded data, 1 `.psp` source), 23 reachable only on legacy paths [B:LEGACY], 17 only in the Direct3D build [B:D3D] (the 14 shaders and 3 deform tables), 10 only in debug/cheat builds [B:DEBUG], 1 only in the editor [B:EDITOR] (`data/shapes/camera.shp`) — every one enumerated in §25.9. All 133 `.max` files are authoring sources that the game never reads.
- **"Referenced [B:FULL]" means compiled and callable, not necessarily seen**: 52 of the 544 are consumed only by building classes that no live Multiwinia map places (shown as "MP-map placements 0" in the Consumer column, counts from `OUT/_work/drafts/P06_level_building_usage.csv`). Some are created at run time (Capture-the-Statue statues `carry*.shp`, crates' `icons/target.bmp`, triffids, master spawn points [I]); the rest are Darwinia-only buildings (`blueprint*`, `bridge*`, `cave`, `chesspiece`, `clonelab`, `constructionyard*`, `controltower*`, `factory`, `feedingtube`, `fenceswitch`, `generator`, `goddish`, `incubator`, `library`, `mine*`, `powerstation`, `*upgradeport`/`upgrade_port`, `receiverlink`, `refinery`, `researchitem`, `spirit*`, `tracklink`, `wheel`, `fuelpipe`, `textures/beam.bmp`, `textures/fuel.bmp`) whose assets are practically unreachable [I] (U5).
- **NPOT is common**: all 57 thumbnails (400×300), 49 of 51 help images, 27 icons, 23 textures, 11 animation bitmaps, 9 waves ramps (400×10, CPU-only). These are rescaled up to the next power of two at upload whenever power-of-two textures are required — always in the D3D build (`code/lib/bitmap.cpp:BitmapRGBA::ConvertToTexture`) [C].
- **Three transparency mechanisms** coexist: magenta (255,0,255) colour key (default `GetTexture` mask flag — 16 files actually contain key pixels), green-channel-as-alpha (`GetTextureWithAlpha` — 4 textures), and true 32-bit BGRA alpha (49 files with a non-trivial alpha channel); most glow/beam art instead relies on additive blending over a black background (§25.12).
- **The texture cache ignores load flags**: seven textures are requested with conflicting flags, so the first requester decides mip-mapping, masking or green-to-alpha conversion for every later user — including `data/icons/marker_colour.bmp`, which is requested both with green-to-alpha (`MarkerSystem::RenderColourCircle`) and as a normal masked texture (`TaskManagerInterfaceMultiwinia::RenderRecentRunTask`) (§25.2.4).
- **Packaging differs from the tree**: 160 in-scope files (133 `.max` + 27 others) are not packed into any archive by the retail script (all `.max`, legacy fonts, Xbox/Ambrosia/MSN branding, plusmenu, `mwhelp/360_controller.bmp`, TankBattle/benchmark thumbnails, `mp_cts_3p_3` map + thumbnail, and every unicode atlas not listed by a language list — notably all three currency atlases `*.20a0.20af.bmp`, which therefore never reach players) (§25.3).
- **Missing files that compiled FULL code requests**: `icons/compass_empty.bmp` is bound by the live `MarkerSystem::RenderCompass` when a marker direction is exactly (0,0) — that `GetTexture` would hit the fatal `AppReleaseAssert("Failed to load texture …")` [C code path; whether an archive supplies the file is U]. All other missing names sit on legacy/dead paths or behind `DoesTextureExist` (§25.8).
- **Shapes**: 139 meshes, 441 fragments, 150 820 triangles, 425 markers; 98 use explicit triangle lists, 39 triangle strips, 2 contain markers only and no fragment (`data/shapes/battlecannonbase.shp`, `data/shapes/portal.shp`). Maximum hierarchy depth 6; one file (`data/shapes/carrycube.shp`) has a duplicate fragment name (`Box04`). 113 shapes are used in FULL; 22 are unused (§25.9).

## [→ 25_textures_and_assets] 25.2 Texture loading pipeline (verified against code; extends P12 §25.1–25.3)

P12 documents the decoder, upload and cache; the points below were re-verified for this catalogue and add what the asset inventory needs.

### 25.2.1 Request → texture, step by step [C] `code/lib/resource.cpp:Resource::GetTextureWithFlags`, `ProcessBmp`; `code/lib/bitmap.cpp`
1. Cache lookup in `m_textures` (a `hash_map<std::string, TextureInfo*>`) keyed by the **exact string passed** (not lower-cased, flags not part of the key).
2. Else lookup in the registered-bitmap store `m_bitmaps` (run-time bitmaps, and any bitmap previously fetched with `GetBitmap`); `ProcessBmp` mutates that stored bitmap in place.
3. Else read the file: name copied and lower-cased → `GetBinaryReader` → loose `data/<name>` first, then the in-RAM archive entry `data/<name>` (archive entries are lower-cased at `ParseArchive`).
4. `ProcessBmp(flags)`: if `WithMask` (0x2) `ConvertPinkToTransparent` (exact (255,0,255) → (128,128,128,0)); if `WithAlpha` (0x4) `ConvertColourToAlpha` (A := G, RGB := 255); upload with or without mip-maps; `WithCompression` (0x8) is passed through only in the D3D build and **no call site requests it** [C grep].
5. Not found → `AppReleaseAssert(false, "Failed to load texture %s")` — fatal.
6. `TextureInfo{id, width, height}` keeps the **source** size, so `GetTextureInfo` (used by the font atlas code) reports pre-rescale dimensions.

`GetTexture(name, mip=true, mask=true, compressed=false)`; `GetTextureWithAlpha(name)` = `WithAlpha|WithMipmaps` (no mask). `DoesTextureExist` checks cache, bitmap store, then file existence (does not load). `GetBitmap(name)` returns the decoded CPU bitmap (bitmap store first, else decodes the file and **stores it in `m_bitmaps` under the name as given**) — used for terrain ramps (`LandscapeRenderer`, `Water`), icon size queries (`ControlHelpSystem`, `MultiwiniaHelp`), animation images (`AnimatedPanelRenderer`) and map saving (`MapFile::SaveTexture`).

### 25.2.2 Decoding constraints that the asset set must respect [C] `code/lib/bitmap.cpp:BitmapRGBA::LoadBmp`
| Constraint | Consequence | Shipped files violating it |
|---|---|---|
| Only BMP (`Initialise` asserts the extension; `TEXTURE_EXTENSION` = "bmp") | PNG is write-only (screenshots) | none |
| Info header 40 (Windows) or 12 (OS/2) bytes; V4/V5 headers hit `AppDebugAssert(0)` and leave the header uninitialised in release | export as classic BITMAPINFOHEADER | none (all 667 have 40) |
| `BI_RGB` only (debug assert) | RLE decodes as garbage in release | `data/textures/shaman_background.bmp` (RLE8; legacy consumer) |
| 4/8-bit rows read without skipping padding | widths must be multiples of 8 (4-bit) / 4 (8-bit) | none |
| Pixel data assumed to follow the palette immediately (no seek to `bfOffBits`) | gaps between palette and pixels corrupt the image | none |
| Rows stored bottom-up and kept in that order (`m_lines[0]` = bottom row) | matches GL texture origin; UI code uses `glTexCoord2i(0,1)` at the top-left vertex | — |
| Palette alpha forced to 255; 24-bit alpha 255; 32-bit = BGRA | only 32-bit files carry alpha | — |

### 25.2.3 Upload, power-of-two and size rules [C] `code/lib/bitmap.cpp:BitmapRGBA::ConvertToTexture`, `code/lib/math_utils.cpp:RequirePowerOfTwoTextures`, `nearestPowerOfTwo`
- GL build: power-of-two required iff `GL_ARB_texture_non_power_of_two` is absent **or** preference `ManuallyScaleTextures` ≠ 0; square required iff `GL_ARB_texture_rectangle` is absent. D3D build: power-of-two always, square never.
- If required, new size = next power of two ≥ each **original** dimension (overrides the max-size downscale computed just before); the image is resampled with bilinear `Blit` and uploaded at that size. A 400×300 thumbnail therefore becomes 512×512 (×2.18 texels); `data/mwhelp/mode_*.bmp` 300×200 → 512×256.
- Wrap S/T = `GL_CLAMP`, MAG = `GL_LINEAR`, MIN = `GL_LINEAR_MIPMAP_LINEAR` are written into every new texture object; mip chains are built with `gluBuild2DMipmaps` (RGBA8) when requested, otherwise level 0 only (`glTexImage2D`, internal format 4). Callers of non-mip-mapped textures reset MIN to LINEAR/NEAREST at draw time, with the exceptions `textures/laser.bmp` in `Location::RenderWeapons` and `code/worldobject/weapons.cpp:Laser::Render` (both request mip=false and set no MIN filter); because the cache ignores flags, whichever request comes first decides (25.2.4).
- Off-main-thread requests are marshalled to the render thread (`ConvertToTextureAsync` → loading-screen job).

### 25.2.4 Order-dependent results: textures requested with conflicting flags [C] `X2a_refs.csv`
Because the cache key is the name only, the first request fixes the processing for the whole session (until `Resource::FlushOpenGlState`):

| Texture | Requests in FULL (flags) | Effect of the order |
|---|---|---|
| `data/textures/laser.bmp` | `GameMenuWindow::PreloadTextures` (start-up) default; `Clouds::RenderSky`, building `RenderAlphas`, `Darwinian::Render` mip+mask; `Location::RenderWeapons`, `Laser::Render` **no mip** | start-up preload wins → mip-mapped, so the no-mip callers that leave MIN = `LINEAR_MIPMAP_LINEAR` still get a complete texture [I]; after a context flush the first requester decides again |
| `data/textures/clouds.bmp` | `Clouds::RenderBlobby` no-mip; `Clouds::RenderFlat`, `GameMenuWindow::PreloadTextures` default | `PreloadTextures` (start-up) wins → mip-mapped (P12 assumed the opposite order); both cloud passes set MIN NEAREST anyway |
| `data/textures/interface_grey.bmp`, `data/textures/interface_red.bmp` | `TaskManagerInterfaceIcons` ctor (built at start-up by `App::Initialise` → `TaskManagerInterface::CreateTaskManager` while the game mode is still None) and `TaskManagerInterfaceMultiwinia` ctor: mip, **no mask**; building/Eclipse renders default (mask) | start-up request wins → unmasked; the files contain no pink pixels, so no visible difference |
| `data/icons/compass.bmp` | `TaskManagerInterfaceMultiwinia::RenderCompass` no mask; `MarkerSystem::RenderCompass` mask | no pink pixels → no visible difference |
| `data/icons/icon_shadow.bmp` | task-manager constructors (start-up) no mask; render calls mask | start-up wins; no pink pixels → no visible difference |
| `data/icons/marker_colour.bmp` | `MarkerSystem::RenderColourCircle`, `TaskManagerInterfaceMultiwinia::RenderRunningTasks` **green→alpha**; `TaskManagerInterfaceMultiwinia::RenderRecentRunTask` normal masked | **visible**: whichever is drawn first decides whether the circle is a white alpha mask (tinted by `glColor`) or the original RGB art. [I] In practice markers render before the task manager every frame (pass 16 before 23, P12 §24.2), so the alpha form wins |

A new engine should key the cache by (name, processing flags) or make processing a property of the asset.

### 25.2.5 Re-upload / context loss [C] `Resource::FlushOpenGlState`, `RegenerateOpenGlState`
All GL textures are deleted and forgotten on flush; registered bitmaps in `m_bitmaps` survive, so run-time textures (§25.10) are rebuilt from their stored bitmaps on next request; file textures are re-read (loose files override archives — the only "hot reload"). Fonts are rebuilt immediately (`BuildUnicodeArray`).

## [→ 25_textures_and_assets] 25.3 Resource archives and their packing

### 25.3.1 Archives opened at start-up [C] `code/app.cpp:App::App`, `App::Initialise`, `App::LoadSounds`; `code/lib/resource.cpp:Resource::ParseArchive`
Order: `init.dat` → `language.dat` → `main.dat` → `patch.dat` → `menusounds.dat` → `branding.dat` → `sounds.dat` → `sounds2.dat`. Each RAR archive is fully decompressed into RAM; entry names are lower-cased; an entry in a later archive replaces an earlier one; a missing archive is silently skipped. Loose files under `data/` always win over archive entries (`GetBinaryReader`, `GetTextReader`, `GetShapeCopy`, `DoesShapeExist`).

### 25.3.2 What the retail packaging script puts where [C] `tools/build_multiwinia_data.bat`, `tools/listfiles.pl`, `tools/copyfiles.pl`
| Archive | Built from | Visual assets it receives |
|---|---|---|
| `init.dat` | `tools/init.list` (copied, then `textures/xboxlivearcade.bmp` deleted on PC) | all 14 `data/shaders/*`, `data/sprites/darwinian.bmp`, `data/textures/ivlogo.bmp` — the loading screen needs these before `main.dat` is parsed (`code/loading_screen.cpp:LoadingScreen::RenderIVLogo`, `RenderFrame`) |
| `language.dat` | retail with no language argument → `tools/language.list` (all languages); `build_multiwinia.bat`/`_gold.bat` (non-retail platform) → `tools/englishlanguage.list`; `retail <lang>` → that language's list | font atlases: all-language list = `editor/speccy/square.0020.00ff`, `speccy.0410.042f`, `speccy.0430.044f` and the 127 CJK/Hangul/kana `square.*` atlases; `russianlanguage.list` adds the Cyrillic `editor.*` and `square.*` atlases; **no list contains `*.20a0.20af.bmp`** |
| `main.dat` | every file under `data/` not matched by `tools/sounds.txt` + `tools/menu_sounds.txt` + `tools/multiwinia_exclusions.txt`, not under `sounds/`, and with extension `tex bmp jpg shp txt vs ps ps3 h anim` | 497 in-scope files (8 of them — the non-FixedFunc shaders and `globals.h` — also in init.dat) |
| `patch.dat`, `branding.dat` | no Multiwinia script in `tools/` builds them [C grep]; `branding.dat` appears only commented out in `tools/InstallerScript_retail_crashreporter_euro.iss` | [I] distributor overlays; `data/shapes/carrybillboard_*` (`pcg`, `tpcg`, `xfire`, `zone`) are unreferenced variants of `carrybillboard.shp`, and `code/worldobject/statue.cpp:Statue::Render` draws an optional `carryspecialshape.shp` if any archive provides it (`DoesShapeExist`) — the likely delivery route for promotional billboard statues [I] |

In-scope exclusions from `tools/multiwinia_exclusions.txt` [C]: `shapes/source`, `textures/editor_font*`, `textures/speccy_font*`, `textures/unicode` (handled by language.dat), `shaders/FixedFunc*` (6 files; they still ship in init.dat), `sprites/darwinian.bmp` and `textures/ivlogo.bmp` (in init.dat), `textures/xboxlivearcade.bmp`, `textures/ambrosia_logo.bmp`, `textures/plusmenu/*`, `textures/msn_oberon_combosplash.bmp`, `textures/dmacrew.bmp`, `textures/speccy_screen.bmp`, `mwhelp/360_controller.bmp`, `thumbnails/mp_benchmark_*`, `thumbnails/mp_tankbattle_*`, `thumbnails/mp_cts_3p_3.bmp` (with `levels/mp_cts_3p_3.txt`). `data/icons/banner_template.psp` is dropped by the extension filter. The `deform*`/`water-meshwaves*`/`globals.h` shaders are in both init.dat and main.dat (identical copies; the later archive's entry wins).

Per-file archive membership is the **Packed** column of §25.5/§25.6.

## [→ 25_textures_and_assets] 25.5 Per-file catalogue — bitmaps, animation scripts and shaders

Columns: **Size** W×H px as stored; **Fmt** BMP bit depth (P = palettised) — every file has a 40-byte BITMAPINFOHEADER and BI_RGB compression unless noted; **Alpha/key** = alpha channel in file (32-bit only), exact (255,0,255) colour-key pixels (made transparent only when loaded with the mask flag), large pure-black area (additive-blend art); **P2** power-of-two both axes; **Packed**; **Status**; **Consumer → pass** (pass numbers = P12 §24.2 frame table); **Load site(s)** first sites in the best build; **Flags** = arguments of the load call (`Resource::GetTexture` defaults mip=true mask=true) and GL state set within ±14 lines of the site ([I] heuristic window).

## [→ 25_textures_and_assets] 25.6 Per-file catalogue — shapes

Columns: **Frags** fragments excluding the synthetic `SceneRoot`; **Depth** deepest fragment level (1 = child of SceneRoot); **Pos/Verts/Tris** summed over fragments (triangles after strip→triangle conversion with degenerate removal, exactly as `ShapeFragment::ParseStripBlock`); **Geom** `triangles` (explicit list) or `strips`; **Markers** count; **Looked-up** fragment/marker names the consuming code fetches by name (`LookupFragment`/`LookupMarker` literals in the consumer files that exist in this shape). Hierarchies and marker lists follow in §25.7.

## [→ 25_textures_and_assets] 25.8 Names requested by code/data that have no file in `data/`

A request for a missing texture through `Resource::GetTexture*` is fatal (`AppReleaseAssert("Failed to load texture %s")`, `code/lib/resource.cpp:Resource::GetTextureWithFlags`) unless the call is guarded by `DoesTextureExist`; a missing shape is fatal in `Resource::GetShapeCopy`. Runtime-generated names (registered with `Resource::AddBitmap`) are listed in §25.10 and excluded here. [C] unless marked.

| Requested name(s) | Site | Compiled in | Reachability note | How built |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `icons/compass_empty.bmp` | `code/markersystem.cpp:MarkerSystem::RenderCompass` | FULL | LIVE: bound when the marker direction is exactly (0,0); unguarded GetTexture -> fatal AppReleaseAssert unless an archive supplies the file [U]; RenderCompassEmpty itself has no caller | GetTexture |
| `icons/compass_empty.bmp` | `code/markersystem.cpp:MarkerSystem::RenderCompassEmpty` | FULL | LIVE: bound when the marker direction is exactly (0,0); unguarded GetTexture -> fatal AppReleaseAssert unless an archive supplies the file [U]; RenderCompassEmpty itself has no caller | GetTexture |
| `mwhelp/darwinian.bmp` | `code/multiwiniahelp.cpp:MultiwiniaHelp::LoadIcons` | FULL | HelpDarwinians tab: its "appropriate" test is hard-coded false (P19), so the icon is never bound |  |

## [→ 25_textures_and_assets] 25.10 Run-time-generated textures and registered bitmaps

Names registered with `Resource::AddBitmap` behave exactly like files (found by `GetTexture`/`DoesTextureExist`/`GetBitmap`; survive context loss and are re-uploaded on demand). `AddBitmap` ignores a name that already exists. [C] unless marked.

| Key (texture name) | Built by | Source → processing | Size | Build / reach | Drawn by |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `sprites/viriifull.bmp` | `code/team.cpp:Team::Initialise` (once, guarded by `DoesTextureExist`) | `sprites/virii.bmp` (32×32) vertically flipped and bilinear-scaled into a black 160×512 canvas: copy *i* has side `32 − i/2` (integer division), centred on x = 16, stacked from y = 0 while `destY + side < 512` → 18 copies (32,32,31,31,…,24,24 px; 504 px tall); then `textures/glow.bmp` 128×128 blitted at (32,0) | 160×512 (NPOT width → 256×512 when rescaled) | [B:FULL] | `code/team.cpp:Team::RenderVirii` (pass 7c) — the tail sprites sample the shrinking copies, the head glow samples the right-hand block [I] |
| `shadow_icons/selectionarrow.bmp` (+ `icons/selectionarrow.bmp` itself) | `code/gamecursor.cpp:GameCursor::GameCursor` | file read with `GetBinaryReader`, registered unchanged, then `ApplyBlurFilter(10)` and registered as `shadow_…` | source size | [B:FULL] | `code/gamecursor.cpp` selection-arrow rendering (binds at ~L1989/L2011, pass 20) — shadow pass `SRC_ALPHA, ONE_MINUS_SRC_COLOR` then additive main pass |
| `shadow_<cursor>` for each `MouseCursor`: `icons/mouse_game.bmp`, `mouse_placement.bmp`, `mouse_disabled.bmp`, `mouse_movehere.bmp`, `mouse_highlight.bmp`, `mouse_turrettarget.bmp`, `mouse_selection.bmp`, `mouse_enter.bmp` (+ the originals) | `code/gamecursor.cpp:MouseCursor::MouseCursor` | as above (blur ×10) | source size | [B:FULL] | `MouseCursor::Render` (pass 20) |
| `shadow_icons/nukesymbol.bmp` | `code/worldobject/nuke.cpp:Nuke::RenderGroundMarker` | blur ×10 of `icons/nukesymbol.bmp` | 128×128 | [B:FULL] | same function |
| `shadow_icons/target.bmp` | `code/worldobject/crate.cpp:Crate::RenderGroundMarker` | blur ×10 of `icons/target.bmp` | 128×128 | [B:FULL] | same function |
| `<flag file>_255`, `<flag file>_selected_<colourId>_<coopPos>`, `<flag file>_<colourId>_<coopPos>` | `code/worldobject/flag.cpp:Flag::Render` (multiplayer only) | `ConvertRedChannel(c)`: pure-red pixels (g = b = 0, r > 0) → `c·r/255`; c = (128,128,128) for no team, full team colour when selected/highlighted, team colour/2 otherwise | source size | [B:FULL] | officer banners `icons/banner_*.bmp` (`Officer::RenderFlag`), Blitzkrieg zone flags `icons/flag*.bmp` (`MultiwiniaZone::RenderBlitzkrieg`) |
| `water_lightmap` (`LIGHTMAP_TEXTURE_NAME`) | `code/water.cpp:Water::GenerateLightMap` | grey = clamp(landMask · 855, 0, 255), row-flipped; old bitmap/texture deleted first | 128×128 (`MASK_SIZE`) | [B:FULL] | `Water::RenderFlatWater`, texture unit 1 (`COMBINE_EXT`) |
| terrain bitmaps of custom maps (key = stored file name, e.g. `terrain/landscape_x.bmp`) | `code/mapfile.cpp:MapFile` texture loader (`AddBitmap(*_newFilename, *bitmap)`) | raw RGBA pixel blob + width/height from the `.mwm` directory | as saved | [B:FULL] | `LandscapeRenderer`/`Water` like a file. `MapFile::SaveTextures` embeds a terrain bitmap only when it is not an archive entry (`IsSaveRequired`) |
| gesture overlay textures (no name) | `code/taskmanager_interface_icons.cpp`, `code/taskmanager_interface_gestures.cpp` (`BITMAPW` = 64) | `BitmapRGBA` drawn on the CPU, `ConvertToTexture(false)` / `glTexImage2D` | 64×64 | [B:LEGACY] | single-player task managers |
| pixel-effect capture | `code/renderer.cpp:Renderer::PreRenderPixelEffect` | `glCopyTexImage2D` RGBA | `m_pixelSize`² (256) | [B:LEGACY] (skipped when `App::Multiplayer()`, P12 §24.2) | `ApplyPixelEffect` |
| menu-transition capture | `code/renderer.cpp:Renderer::CaptureFrameBuffer` | `glCopyTexImage2D` RGB of the back buffer | screen size or next power of two of it (D3D/GL difference handled in code) | [B:FULL] | `AdvanceMenuTransition` (pass 34) |
| deform render targets | `code/deform.cpp:DeformEffect::DeformEffect` | `Texture::Create(TF_RENDERTARGET)`: `m_screenTexture` (screen size), `m_deformTexture` (half size, `D3DFMT_A8R8G8B8`) | screen / half | [B:D3D]; effect never runs in a match (P12) | DeformEffect |
| water reflection target | `code/water_reflection.cpp:WaterReflectionEffect` | `Texture::Create` half-screen, back-buffer format, `TF_RENDERTARGET` | half screen | [B:D3D] | `water-meshwaves` shader |
| legacy font up-scale | `code/lib/text_renderer.cpp:TextRenderer::BuildOpenGlState` | font bitmap ×2 nearest | 2× | [B:DEAD in practice] (no font name ends in `.bmp`) | — |
| Vista rich-save thumbnail | `code/app.cpp:App::SaveThumbnailScreenshot` | `PosterMaker` frame → bilinear 256×192 → PNG in the save header | 256×192 | [B:DEAD] (`TARGET_OS_VISTA`) | — |

Names that the code checks with `DoesTextureExist` before building (viriifull, shadows, flag variants) are built once per process and reused across matches; they are never freed except by `Water::GenerateLightMap` (lightmap only).

## [→ 25_textures_and_assets] 25.11 Cross-check with `tools/FindUnusedResources.sh`

Emulation of the shell tool (same grep/sed pipeline: only lines containing `GetShape(`/`GetSound(`/`GetTexture(` *and* a quote, last call on the line, directory stripped, compared case-insensitively by **basename** against every `.bmp/.shp/.ogg/.jpg/.wav` under `data/`, exception regexes from `tools/FindUnusedResourcesExceptions.txt`: `editor_font.bmp`, `landscape.bmp`, `viriifull.bmp`). The tool also counts commented-out code and cannot see names built with `sprintf`, `DoesTextureExist`-guarded paths, arrays, or data-file references, so it disagrees with this catalogue as follows (in-scope visual assets only).

## [→ 25_textures_and_assets] 25.12 Texture usage conventions (what an artist/engine must reproduce)

| Convention | Mechanism | Assets (in FULL) | Ref |
|---|---|---|---|
| Magenta colour key | default `GetTexture(…, mask=true)`: exact (255,0,255) → (128,128,128,0); grey RGB avoids pink fringes under bilinear filtering and mip-mapping | 16 files contain key pixels: `data/sprites/darwinian.bmp`, `darwinian_ouch1.bmp`, `darwinian_ouch2.bmp`, `darwinian_ouch3.bmp`, `darwinian_ouch4.bmp`, `brainwaves.bmp`, `santahat.bmp`, `lasertrooper.bmp` (unused), `sound.bmp` (debug); `data/textures/clouds.bmp`, `shapewireframe.bmp`, `dmacrew.bmp`, `program_darwinia.bmp`, `speccy_screen.bmp` (the last three dead); `data/animations/darwinian.bmp`, `darwinian_ouch4.bmp`. Every other file requested with the mask flag simply has no magenta pixels | `code/lib/bitmap.cpp:ConvertPinkToTransparent` |
| Alpha test instead of blending for sprites | Darwinians drawn with `GL_ALPHA_TEST GREATER 0.04`, units 0.02 | `data/sprites/darwinian*.bmp`, `futurewinian.bmp` | P12 §24.2 passes 7a/7d |
| Monochrome mask as alpha | `GetTextureWithAlpha`: A := green, RGB := white, tinted by `glColor` | `data/textures/tree.bmp` (tree canopy), `data/icons/compass_shadow.bmp`, `marker_shadow.bmp`, `marker_colour.bmp` | `code/lib/bitmap.cpp:ConvertColourToAlpha`, `code/lib/resource.cpp:GetTextureWithAlpha` |
| True alpha channel | 32-bit BGRA files — 49, every one with a non-trivial alpha channel: `data/textures/darwinian.bmp`, `mwtitle.bmp`, `prices.bmp`, `prices_ambrosia.bmp`, `prices_steam.bmp`; `data/sprites/futurewinian.bmp`; 19 of the 25 `data/animations/*.bmp` (all except `darwinian.bmp`, `darwinian_ouch4.bmp`, `flag*.bmp` ×4); 21 icons (`arrow`, `button_a`, `button_a_hold`, `button_b`, `button_lb`, `button_tab`, `button_x`, `button_y`, `menu_advanced`, `menu_basic`, `menu_intermediate`, `mouse_disabled`, `mouse_lmb`, `mouse_lmb_hold`, `mouse_rmb`, `mouse_turrettarget`, `mwlogo`, `rocketstatuspanel`, `thickarrow`, `tick`, `widearrow`); `data/mwhelp/360_controller.bmp`, `control_camera.bmp`, `control_camera_french.bmp` | drawn with `SRC_ALPHA, ONE_MINUS_SRC_ALPHA` | §25.5 Alpha column |
| Additive glow on black | art painted on black, drawn with `SRC_ALPHA, ONE` so black contributes nothing; alpha comes from `glColor` | `starburst`, `glow`, `cloudyglow`, `laser`, `laser-long`, `laserfence*`, `beam`, `bubble`, `lightning`, `muzzleflash`, `subversion`, `fuel`, `radarsignal`, `particle` (glow batch), `triangleoutline` (landscape grid), cloud textures, unicode font atlases (text is additive, P12 §24.7) | P12 §24.2 |
| Dark halo / drop shadow | `SRC_ALPHA, ONE_MINUS_SRC_COLOR` with colour `(a,a,a,0)` darkens by the texel brightness: used with blurred `shadow_*` copies, `icon_shadow.bmp`, `*_shadow.bmp` button art and the text outline pass | cursor, markers, task manager | `code/gamecursor.cpp:MouseCursor::Render`, `code/markersystem.cpp:MarkerSystem::RenderShadow` |
| Team colouring of 2D art | (a) vertex colour modulation (`glColor` × white/grey art: Darwinian sprites, icons, markers); (b) baked variant via `ConvertRedChannel` for flags (§25.10) — only pure-red pixels are recoloured, so flag art must use pure red for the team area | `icons/banner_*.bmp`, `icons/flag*.bmp` | `code/worldobject/flag.cpp:Flag::Render` |
| Team colouring of meshes | palette entries with `r > 2g && r > 2b` replaced by brightness-preserving team colour (root + direct children only); see P12 §24.5 | all team-owned `.shp` (red parts are the team parts) | `code/worldobject/entity.cpp:ConvertShapeColoursToTeam` |
| Ramps are not textures | `terrain/landscape_*.bmp` (64×64 colour ramp, height × slope lookup) and `terrain/waves_*.bmp` (400×10; row 1 used as a per-x colour table) are read on the CPU with `GetBitmap`; only `terrain/water_*.bmp` (128×128) is uploaded | 36 terrain files | P11 §23.11, `code/water.cpp:Water::Water`, `RenderFlatWater` |
| UI pictures keep exact pixels | front-end images are requested **without mip-maps** (`thumbnails/*`, `mwhelp/mode_*`, `mwhelp/general*`, `mwhelp/help_*`, `textures/mwtitle.bmp`, `textures/prices.bmp`, `textures/multiwinia.bmp`) and drawn with MIN/MAG LINEAR | §25.13 | `code/UI/ImageButton.cpp:ImageButton::Render`, `MapImageButton.h`, `GameTypeImageButton.h` |
| Size conventions | icons 128×128 or 64×64 (some 50×50 / 100×50 / 128×64), sprites 32–128 px squares, VFX strips 128×32 / 64×256, thumbnails 400×300 (8-bit palettised except 4), mode pictures 300×200, help images 150×150 / 200×200, font atlases 512 px wide | — | §25.5 |
| Christmas variant | when `Location::ChristmasModEnabled()`: terrain forced to `landscape_icecaps`/`waves_earth`/`water_icecaps`, Darwinians get `sprites/santahat.bmp` | — | `code/landscape_renderer.cpp:LandscapeRenderer::LandscapeRenderer`, `code/water.cpp`, `code/worldobject/darwinian.cpp:Darwinian::Render` |

## [→ 25_textures_and_assets] 25.13 How mip-map, wrap and filter are chosen per load site

There is no per-asset metadata: every choice is made by the requesting call (mip/mask/alpha flags) and by GL state the drawing code sets after binding. Defaults written at upload (every texture): wrap CLAMP/CLAMP, MAG LINEAR, MIN LINEAR_MIPMAP_LINEAR (`code/lib/bitmap.cpp:BitmapRGBA::ConvertToTexture`); the per-frame default state (P12 §24.1) adds env MODULATE. Per-site overrides found within the drawing code [C] (sites in `X2a_refs.csv`, column `gl_state`, collected from the ±14 lines around each bind — a heuristic window, so confirm in the cited function before relying on an individual entry [I]):

| Choice | Assets | Where set |
|---|---|---|
| mip-mapped (default `GetTexture`) | all world-space art: building/unit glows, VFX, icons drawn in 3D or scaled HUD, font atlases | every `GetTexture(name)` with default args |
| **no mip-maps** + MIN LINEAR at draw | the 51 thumbnails used in FULL (+3 TankBattle, debug), `mwhelp/mode_*` (7 pre-loaded; prologue/campaign legacy), `mwhelp/general1–5`, `mwhelp/help_*` (16), `textures/mwtitle.bmp`, `textures/prices.bmp`, `textures/multiwinia.bmp` | `MapImageButton::Render`, `GameMenuWindow::PreloadThumbnails`, `GameTypeImageButton::Render`, `GameMenuWindow::Setup*Page` preloads, `ImageButton::Render`, `GameMenuWindow::Render` (title) |
| no mip-maps, NEAREST | `textures/clouds.bmp` (blobby clouds: MAG LINEAR / MIN NEAREST; flat clouds NEAREST/NEAREST) | `code/clouds.cpp:Clouds::RenderBlobby`, `RenderFlat` |
| no mip-maps, MIN left at default | `textures/laser.bmp` in `Location::RenderWeapons` and `Laser::Render` (saved by the start-up preload — §25.2.4) | `code/location.cpp`, `code/worldobject/weapons.cpp` |
| mip-maps, MAG NEAREST (crisp pixels) | `textures/particle.bmp` (particle batches; restored to LINEAR after), `sprites/darwinian.bmp` for the giant front-end Darwinian (restored to LINEAR after) | `code/particle_system.cpp:ParticleSystem::Render`, `code/UI/GameMenuWindow.cpp:GameMenuWindow::RenderBigDarwinian` |
| NEAREST both (pixel-exact UI strip) | `textures/interface_divider.bmp` (legacy task managers) | `code/taskmanager_interface_icons.cpp`, `_gestures.cpp` |
| **REPEAT** wrap (tiled / scrolling UVs) | `textures/clouds.bmp`, `cloudyglow.bmp` (front-end fill boxes), `fuel.bmp`, `interface_blur.bmp`, `interface_grey.bmp`, `interface_red.bmp`, `laser-long.bmp`, `laser.bmp` (sky grid), `laserfence.bmp`, `laserfence2.bmp`, `menuhighlight.bmp`, `radarsignal.bmp`, `triangleoutline.bmp` (landscape overlay), all `terrain/water_*.bmp` (flat water, unit 0), `icons/mwlogo.bmp`, `icons/thickarrow.bmp` | the drawing functions listed for each asset in §25.5 (e.g. `LandscapeRenderer::RenderOverlaySlow`, `Water::RenderFlatWater`, `LaserFence::RenderAlphas`, `RadarDish::RenderSignal`, `DrawFillBox`, `RenderHighlightBeam`); the functions inspected restore CLAMP afterwards [I for the rest] |
| env DECAL | `textures/shapewireframe.bmp` on explosion debris | `code/explosion.cpp:ExplosionManager::Render` |
| env `COMBINE_EXT` with RGB `REPLACE` | `textures/laserfence.bmp` + `radarsignal.bmp` signal beams (two-texture) | `code/worldobject/radardish.cpp:RadarDish::RenderSignal`, `code/worldobject/feedingtube.cpp:FeedingTube::RenderSignal` |
| multitexture COMBINE | `water_lightmap` on unit 1 over `terrain/water_*.bmp` | `Water::RenderFlatWater` |
| unmasked (`mask=false`) | `textures/triangleoutline.bmp`, `interface_grey/red.bmp`, `icons/compass.bmp`, `gestureguide.bmp`, `icon_shadow.bmp`, all thumbnails, `mwhelp/mode_*`, `sepulveda/*` (current frame), `textures/deform*.bmp` | call arguments |
| green→alpha | `textures/tree.bmp`, `icons/compass_shadow.bmp`, `marker_shadow.bmp`, `marker_colour.bmp` | `GetTextureWithAlpha` |
| D3D sampler states set explicitly | deform tables: LINEAR, U WRAP / V CLAMP | `code/deform.cpp:DeformEffect` (P12 §24) |

Anisotropic filtering, texture compression, sRGB and LOD bias are never used [C grep: no `GL_TEXTURE_MAX_ANISOTROPY`, no compressed request].

## [→ 25_textures_and_assets] 25.14 Fonts and atlases

- **Live fonts** [C] `code/app.cpp:App::SetLanguage`: `g_gameFont = "speccy"`, `g_editorFont = "editor"`, `g_titleFont = "square"`; each is a set of atlases `data/textures/unicode/<font>.<lo>.<hi>.bmp` discovered with `Resource::ListResources("textures/unicode/", "<font>.*.*.bmp")` and loaded with default flags (mip + magenta key) — glyph metrics and UV formulae in P12 §24.7.
- **Atlas inventory** (138 files): Latin-1 `0020–00ff` 512×448 8-bit (3), Cyrillic `0410–042f`, `0430–044f` 512×64 24-bit (6), currency `20a0–20af` 512×32 24-bit (3), kana/CJK punctuation `square.3000.30ff` 512×512 8-bit (1), CJK ideographs `square.4e00.4eff` … `square.9e00.9eff` 256×256 24-bit (81), Hangul `square.ac00.acff` … `square.d700.d7ff` 512×512 4-bit (44, including the mis-named `square.bb00.bfff.bmp`, P12 §24.7).
- **What actually ships** (§25.3): the all-language retail `language.dat` contains Latin-1 for all three fonts, Cyrillic **only for speccy**, all CJK/Hangul for square; the Russian-only build adds Cyrillic for editor and square. The three currency atlases (`*.20a0.20af.bmp`, which cover € and other currency signs) are in **no** list, so a packed build cannot draw U+20A0–U+20AF; in an English-only build (`tools/englishlanguage.list`) only the three Latin-1 atlases exist. Missing atlases simply leave glyphs unmapped (`TextRenderer::GetTextureID` finds no range) [I].
- **Legacy single-bitmap fonts** `data/textures/editor_font*.bmp` (6) and `data/textures/speccy_font_*.bmp` (4): no code requests them (only a commented `GetTexture("textures/editor_font_normal.bmp")` in `code/app.cpp:App::Initialise`), and `tools/multiwinia_exclusions.txt` drops them from packing [C]. `TextRenderer::BuildOpenGlState` (the legacy path) would only be used for a font name ending in `.bmp`, which no caller passes.
- **For a new engine**: replace with a runtime glyph cache over a licensed vector font (one regular + one display face), fixed advance not required; keep the two-pass outline/additive text look (P12 §24.7).

## [→ 25_textures_and_assets] 25.15 Shapes: conventions the catalogue relies on

- Format, hierarchy, transforms, display lists, hit tests and team recolouring: P12 §24.5 (verified; the X2a parser reproduces the engine's triangle counts exactly, including strip winding/degenerate removal).
- Shapes are looked up as `data/shapes/<lower-cased name>` (`Resource::GetShapeCopy`), so code always passes bare names (`"armour.shp"`); per-team copies are cached under synthetic names `<shape>_<r>_<g>_<b>_<a>` (`Entity::SetupTeamShape`), `armour.shp_<colour>_<coop>` (Armour), `battlecannonturret.shp_<colour>_<coop>` and `tank_body.shp_/tank_turret.shp_<colour>_<group>`, `armyant*.shp_<colour>_<group>`, `<head/body>…_<colour>` variants for Centipede/Tentacle/SoulDestroyer — these are `AddShape` keys, not files.
- Level-placed meshes: `StaticShape` buildings name their mesh in the level file (`beachheadobstruction.shp`, `rockhead.shp`, `temple2–4.shp`, `statue.shp`, `spaceship.shp`, `carryspider.shp` in live Multiwinia maps; `temple1.shp` only in the Darwinia map `data/levels/map_temple.txt`).
- Statue (Capture the Statue) mesh is chosen with `syncrand() % 8` from `carrystatue`, `carrysquaddie`, `carryglobe`, `carrytelescope`, `carryweights`, `carrycube`, `carryteapot`, `carrybillboard` (the last enables an optional overlay `carryspecialshape.shp` if a distributor archive supplies one) — `carryspider`/`carrydollar` cases are commented out [C] `code/worldobject/statue.cpp:Statue::Initialise`. Because the choice consumes the synchronised RNG, the statue mesh list is part of the lock-step simulation contract.
- Marker vocabulary (counts over all 139 files): `MarkerPort*` 85, `MarkerLight*` 31, `MarkerIsland*` 31, `MarkerBarrelEnd*` 21, `MarkerStatus*` 20, `MarkerCarry*` 13, `MarkerResearch*` 13, `MarkerLeg*` 11, `MarkerPrimitive*` 9, `MarkerSpike*` 8, `MarkerEngine*` 8, `MarkerTrack*` 8, `MarkerSpiritLink*` 7, `MarkerBarrel*` 6, `MarkerSpiritIncoming*` 6, `MarkerTrunk8to*`/`MarkerTrunk2to*` 5 each, `MarkerWheel*`, `MarkerTail*`, `MarkerFloat*` 5 each, and 40 rarer names (full list per file in §25.7). Code fetches markers/fragments by these literal names (`LookupMarker`, `LookupFragment`; "Looked-up names" column of §25.6), so a replacement asset pipeline must preserve named attachment points.
- `.max` sources (133) pair 1:1 by stem with exported `.shp` for most meshes; the exporter (`tools/ShapeExporter`) is outside this scope.

## Cross-partition notes
- **P12 / 24_rendering**: `ControlHelpSystem::Render` (P12 pass 25) returns immediately when `App::Multiplayer()` and `RenderHelp` requires `App::IsSinglePlayer()` — the pass is empty in every Multiwinia match [C] `code/control_help.cpp:ControlHelpSystem::Render`. The first-request-wins cache order for `textures/clouds.bmp` is the opposite of what P12 §25.3 assumed (the start-up `GameMenuWindow::PreloadTextures` loads it mip-mapped first) [C] `code/app.cpp:App::Initialise`.
- **P17b/P18/P19 (UI)**: help pages and mode pictures confirmed; `TaskManagerInterfaceIcons` is constructed once at start-up (game mode None) and pre-loads textures, although never drawn in a match [C] `code/app.cpp:App::Initialise` → `code/taskmanager_interface.cpp:TaskManagerInterface::CreateTaskManager`.
- **P11 (terrain)**: terrain ramps are CPU-side lookup tables; unused ramps (`landscape_beach/field/icecaps_peaked/ridges/volcanic/volcanicplain`) and old-map-only ramps (`landscape_containment/marble2/mine2`, `waves_desert`) enumerated in §25.9.
- **P14 / packaging docs (29/31)**: the archive composition table in §25.3 and the per-file Packed column are derived from `tools/build_multiwinia_data.bat` semantics; `patch.dat`/`branding.dat` have no build script in `tools/`.
- **P08 (crates)**: crate outcome icons come from fixed literals in `code/worldobject/crate.cpp:Crate::Run*`; the `icon_<Crate::GetName>` pattern is used only by the debug-only crate help window.
- **P15/P22 (determinism)**: `Statue::Initialise` mesh choice consumes `syncrand()` — mesh set must be identical on all peers.

## Contradictions / open questions
- `textures/laser.bmp` and `clouds.bmp` cache order: P12 inferred `RenderBlobby` loads clouds first (non-mip); code shows the start-up preload loads both with default flags first [C]. P12's conclusion for laser ("saved because RenderSky requests it mip-mapped earlier") is right in outcome but for a different reason.
- `icons/compass_empty.bmp` is requested by live code (`MarkerSystem::RenderCompass` when `_screenDX == 0 && _screenDY == 0`, and unconditionally by `MarkerSystem::RenderCompassEmpty`) but the file does not exist in `data/`; `GetTexture` would raise the fatal "Failed to load texture" assert. `RenderCompassEmpty` has no caller [C grep], so only the zero-direction case is exposed.
- `data/textures/shaman_background.bmp` is RLE8-compressed; the loader does not support RLE. Harmless only because ShamanInterface is unreachable in Multiwinia.
- `data/icons/marker_colour.bmp` is processed differently depending on first requester (§25.2.4) — a latent visual inconsistency.
- `data/shapes/carrycube.shp` declares fragment `Box04` twice (debug assert in `Shape::Load`; in release both attach, and `LookupFragment("Box04")` returns the first).
- `data/thumbnails/mp_cts_3p_3.bmp` and the level `mp_cts_3p_3.txt` are both excluded from retail packing although the level is otherwise a normal CTS map — the map is effectively cut from the shipped game [C] `tools/multiwinia_exclusions.txt`.

## [U] Unknowns
- U1: Contents of `patch.dat` and `branding.dat` (e.g. whether `carryspecialshape.shp`, `icons/compass_empty.bmp` or replacement art were delivered there). Resolve: inspect shipped archives of a retail install.
- U2: Whether shipped retail builds used `build_multiwinia_retail.bat` with or without a language argument (decides which unicode atlases each SKU contains). Resolve: release logs or archive listings.
- U3: Exact visual role of the 18 shrinking Virii copies inside `sprites/viriifull.bmp` (UV mapping lives in `Team::RenderVirii`, owned by the worldobject partition). Resolve: read the UV code there.
- U4: Whether GL drivers of the period treated the no-mip textures left with MIN = `LINEAR_MIPMAP_LINEAR` as complete (affects only the `laser.bmp` no-mip callers after a context flush). Resolve: run on period hardware or a strict GL implementation.

- U5: Which of the 52 FULL-referenced assets whose consumers have no placement in live MP maps are actually instantiated at run time (game-mode or crate spawning) — owned by the building/entity partitions (P06/P07/P08). Resolve: cross-reference their spawn analysis; until then treat Darwinia-only building meshes as optional.

## Docs-vs-code discrepancies
- `docs/RussianFontRequest.txt` states that only the Speccy font has Cyrillic glyphs and that Speccy was copied to Square and Editor; `data/textures/unicode/` now contains distinct Cyrillic atlases for all three fonts, but the all-language package list (`tools/language.list`) still ships only the Speccy Cyrillic atlases [C].
- `docs/landscape lightmap.txt` describes `Landscape::ApplyLightmap`/`GenerateLightMap` with `m_lightMapTextureId`; in code the lightmap is generated by `Water::GenerateLightMap` as the registered bitmap `water_lightmap` and `Landscape` only triggers it [C] `code/landscape.cpp`, `code/water.cpp`.
- `docs/OpenGLState.txt` lists MAG/MIN filters as "undefined" in the baseline texture state; every texture object is created with MAG LINEAR / MIN LINEAR_MIPMAP_LINEAR and CLAMP wrap by `BitmapRGBA::ConvertToTexture` [C].
- `tools/FindUnusedResources.sh` (Darwinia-era: it refuses to run unless the working directory is named `Darwinia`) cannot be trusted for Multiwinia: it misses 380 used files and counts 21 unused ones as used (§25.11).

## IP register
All shipped art, meshes, animation scripts, fonts, shaders and file names in scope are Introversion Software IP (or third-party brands). Nothing may be reused; the plan below gives each category a neutral, re-creatable placeholder and naming scheme so that the new team can build an asset list with the same technical roles.

| Item | Kind | Where (ref) | Neutral placeholder |
|---|---|---|---|
| Darwinian / Multiwinian / Futurewinian character sprites (`data/sprites/darwinian*.bmp`, `futurewinian.bmp`, `ghost.bmp`, `santahat.bmp`, `lasertrooper.bmp`, `egg.bmp`, `virii.bmp`, `brainwaves.bmp`, `nuke.bmp`, `sub*.bmp`, `sound.bmp`; `data/animations/darwinian*.bmp`; `data/textures/darwinian.bmp`) | art + character names | `code/worldobject/darwinian.cpp`, `team.cpp`, `souldestroyer.cpp`, `nuke.cpp` | `unit_basic.png`, `unit_hurt_1..4.png`, `unit_side.png`, `unit_alt.png`, `ghost_fx.png`, `hat_seasonal.png`, `creature_tail.png`; generic flat-colour stick-figure style produced in-house |
| Dr Sepulveda portrait frames (`data/sepulveda/sepulveda1–5.bmp`) | character art | `code/clouds.cpp:Clouds::RenderFace` | `advisor_face_idle_1..3`, `_blink`, `_talk` (or drop the feature) |
| Studio and game logos/titles: `data/textures/ivlogo.bmp`, `data/icons/mwlogo.bmp`, `data/textures/mwtitle.bmp`, `data/textures/multiwinia.bmp`, `data/textures/plusmenu/*`, `data/icons/darwin_research_associates.bmp` (in-fiction company "Darwin Research Associates"), `data/textures/program_darwinia.bmp` | brand | loading screen, front-end | `studio_logo_placeholder`, `game_title_wordmark`, `in_fiction_corp_logo` |
| Third-party brands: `data/textures/xboxlivearcade.bmp` (Microsoft), `data/mwhelp/360_controller.bmp` and `data/icons/button_*.bmp` controller glyphs (Xbox 360 trade dress), `data/textures/ambrosia_logo.bmp`, `prices_ambrosia.bmp` (Ambrosia Software), `prices_steam.bmp` (Valve/Steam), `msn_oberon_combosplash.bmp` (MSN/Oberon), `data/shapes/carrybillboard_pcg/_tpcg/_xfire/_zone.shp` and `.max` sources (PC Gamer, XFire, Zone — [I] from the suffixes), `data/textures/dmacrew.bmp`, `speccy_screen.bmp` (demo-scene / ZX Spectrum homage) | third-party brand | various (mostly unused) | do not recreate; platform-neutral input glyphs (`input_glyph_<button>`), no store or magazine branding |

| Map thumbnails and map file names (`data/thumbnails/mp_*.bmp`) | level design art + names | front-end | `map_<mode>_<players>p_<n>_thumb` generated from new maps |
| Help illustrations and mode pictures (`data/mwhelp/*.bmp`), animated help panels (`data/animations/*.anim` + bitmaps) | art + layout | help windows, tutorial overlay | new diagrams per mode (`help_<mode>_<page>`, `mode_<mode>`); re-author panel scripts in a new format |

| VFX and UI textures (`glow`, `starburst`, `cloudyglow`, `laser*`, `beam`, `bubble`, `lightning`, `particle`, `triangleoutline`, `shapewireframe`, `interface_*`, `menuhighlight`, cursor and marker icons, task/crate icons `icon_*.bmp`, `gesture_*.bmp`) | art | §25.5 | regenerate procedurally or redraw: `fx_glow_soft`, `fx_star`, `fx_beam`, `ui_panel_gradient`, `cursor_<state>`, `icon_<ability>` |
